- ~~**Amendment roll-call votes, House half + the latent 500 (HO 532 walk · HO 533 fix)**~~ — **SHIPPED `3c4c72c` (House) · `85d6660` (fix)**, prod-verified on the cache-hit path. Completes the `recordedVotes` gap across both chambers (Senate was HO 530). **HO 532:** an `amendment_votes` link table (composite PK) + an `amendment_vote_walked_at` sentinel on `amendments` (sync-invisible, HO 459 pattern) + a partial `idx_amendments_hamdt` walk-queue index; `sync:amendment-votes --walk` fetches each HAMDT's `/actions`, constructs the deterministic `house-{congress}-{session}-{rollNumber}` vote_id, upserts the link, stamps the sentinel (re-walk keyed on `update_date > walked_at`); a bounded cron walk step in `/api/cron/amendments`. `getBillAmendmentVotes` widened to SAMDT+HAMDT with a new `amendment_votes → votes` join; render unchanged (chamber-agnostic). Backfill 267 HAMDT walked / 92 links / 90 amendments / 0 errors. **HO 533 — two fixes, one blocking:** `getBillAmendmentVotes` returned a `Map`, which `unstable_cache` round-trips to `{}` on a **cache hit**, so `BillAmendments` threw `votes.get is not a function` and 500'd every amendment-bearing `/bill/[id]` ~59min/hr **since HO 530** (hidden because every verify landed in the post-deploy cache-miss window); fixed → plain `Record`. Plus a zero-split guard in `VoteLine` (suppress a false `D 0-0 · R 0-0` when member_votes lag). A repo-wide audit confirmed it was the only Map/Set/Date-returning cached function. **No theme-% change** — linkage completion of the 519 gap + a correctness fix; combined bar holds at 92. See roadmap "Also (HO 532–533)"; oddities (2 process lessons). Docs: HO 534.

N …`) — 114 SAMDT, 100% on tracked bills, `member_votes` present, so query-only, zero backfill; **House needs the walk** (generic question, no number) but `/actions.recordedVotes.rollNumber` resolves 100% to existing `votes` rows, so it's a bounded *linkage* backfill, not a member-vote backfill. `getBillAmendmentVotes(billId)` (a separate cached query, `getBillAmendments` untouched) returns `Map<amendmentId, AmendmentVote[]>` (a list — 3 amendments carry a table+substantive pair); the `/bill/[id]` AMENDMENTS section shows the tally + D/R/I party split, and the display-only disposition dot becomes authoritative from `votes.result` where a vote exists. `votes.id` is a TEXT composite (`senate-119-1-3`). **No theme-% change** — the Senate half of the 519 `recordedVotes` gap; combined bar holds at 92 (House + per-member drill still banked → QUEUED). - ~~**Playwright smoke-crawler CI wiring → Phase 2 Playwright-in-CI**~~ — **CLOSED HO 504 by explicit tombstone with a REPLACED criterion**, not by meeting the original one (the convention's second door — "an item leaves OPEN LOOPS only by meeting its close-criterion or being explicitly tombstoned"). Opened Jun 30 (HO 379), carried through five HOs. **Original criterion:** the e2e job runs green in CI against a **real DB** with the interaction hardenings. **Replaced with:** an unattended **prod** crawl (`.github/workflows/e2e-prod.yml`, HO 504) on `deployment_status` + daily schedule + `workflow_dispatch`, **no fixture, no secrets**, off the per-commit path. **Why:** the HO 503 probe found the specs guard with `test.skip`-on-empty, so an under-seeded fixture **passes while exercising nothing** — false-green, strictly worse than the red-blindness it was meant to cure (oddities) — and pre-merge protection buys minutes in a single-committer flow that already diff-gates + prod-verifies every change, at the cost of a hundreds-of-row artifact maintained forever against a 132-statement schema. The bucket count (73 tests, A 41 · B 28 · C 2 · D 2 · E 0, 95% nominally servable) said build it; the failure-mode analysis said stop, and it was right. **Fallback considered and NOT taken** (recorded so it isn't re-litigated): a few-dozen-row fixture backing **only** the (A) route crawl tagged `@ci`, never the guarded interaction tests — available if pre-merge #418 protection is ever explicitly wanted. **The probe's one open question — does every (A) route 200 on a migrated-but-empty DB, or 500 on a `rows[0]` access — is MOOT under this verdict** (no fixture is built), not a dangling measurement someone should go take. Hardenings: #2 (console allowlist) + #3 (bill selector) shipped in `fc42872`; #1 (freeze-the-marquee) retired as moot-under-BotID. One surviving thread — the `schedule` first-fire — stays in OPEN LOOPS. Detail: HO 503 report + HO 504.
- ~~**`queryEnactedThisWeek` cold-stall → enacted-helper hint (HO 481)**~~ — SHIPPED `9c19557`. The 5th and last known sibling of the HO 405/407/480 OR-lure, flagged-not-touched by the 480 sweep because it's a shared HO 232 helper (the ENACTED THIS WEEK banner + the week-over-week delta both consume it). Hint-first, no migration: `INDEXED BY idx_bills_enacted` on both `queryEnactedThisWeek` and `queryEnactedPriorWeekCount` — the partial index (`WHERE stage='enacted'`) qualifies because the query carries `AND stage='enacted'`, collapsing `MULTI-INDEX OR` over `idx_bills_dash_stage` + temp-btree → `SCAN bills USING INDEX idx_bills_enacted` (cold 617ms / 106ms, well under the 10s `boundedFetch` wall; identical rows/count vs unhinted). Low-severity by construction (the `stage='enacted'` seek keeps it off the full corpus, so it never tipped the wall — closed for hygiene, to shut the known instances of the class). **Corrected premise:** HO 335's assumption that this rode `idx_bills_enacted` cleanly was wrong — that index keys `(congress, latest_action_date)`, has no `stage_changed_at`, so a residual in-memory sort remains (trivial at the enacted row count). See roadmap "Also (HO 481)". Docs: HO 481.
- ~~**`/api/sync` trailing-step timeout → lead-query hint sweep (HO 479 probe / HO 480 fix)**~~ — SHIPPED `b440b82`. **The overrun was mis-diagnosed by its own symptom:** 32 of 40 ticks timed out at the 55s cap, but the trailing step wasn't slow-Gemini (HO 479's hypothesis) — it was the HO 405/407 **cold-DB-stall**. `gatherLeadData`'s transitions query, OR-lured (`is_ceremonial=0 OR IS NULL`) into a `MULTI-INDEX OR` over `idx_bills_is_ceremonial` (~the whole 16.8k corpus) + a temp-btree sort, **ignored the perfect `idx_bills_stage_changed_at`** and cold-stalled past the 10s `boundedFetch` — retrying to 10+10s and *failing* the (non-fatal) lead step, so the dashboard lead had been **silently going stale on every real-delta tick**, worse than the visible timeout implied. HO 479 (read-only probe `scripts/diagnostic/sync-trailing-cost-479.ts`) pinned it; HO 480 swept `gatherLeadData` — `INDEXED BY` on `transRs`/`introRs`/`topicRs`, the 407 remedy applied to the sibling function 407 missed — collapsing lead from the 14–20s stall band to ~1–3s cold, wall now ~33–40s under the 55s cap. **Deferred (own HO):** timeout rows carry no per-step timings — `wrapCronRoute` discards the handler payload on the `Promise.race` reject — a fleet-wide contract change, not needed once the route stops timing out. The 4th lead read (`queryEnactedThisWeek`) mis-plans too → new OPEN LOOP (closed HO 481). See roadmap "Also (HO 480)". Docs: HO 480.
- ~~**Markets watchdog → cron-health watchdog + `/api/health` (HO 477)**~~ — SHIPPED `92baba0`, prod-verified (200 / `healthy:true` / 14 routes). Generalized from markets-only to a fleet-wide check: `lib/cron-health.ts` (`CRON_ROUTES` registry + `computeCronHealth`) feeds a new `/api/health` GET (200/503) + the refreshed HO 135 diagnostic. **Liveness = a `cron_runs` row in the route's window** (success OR timeout — timeout is HO 139's fired-and-executed, not a stop) + error/orphaned as the hard-fail. **Markets keyed on `market_ticks` freshness** (the literal "no `market_ticks` write in N hours" this loop asked for), unhealthy iff ticks > ~9h stale OR error/orphaned — the permanent POLY-SHUTDOWN chronicErr is ignored. Endpoint is unauthed, `force-dynamic`/`no-store`, not `wrapCronRoute`-wrapped, 503s on a check failure. **The push layer is out-of-repo:** point an uptime monitor (UptimeRobot / cron-job.org) at `/api/health`, alert on non-200 — the only thing that catches a silently-stopped cron (Vercel can't; no invocation to error on). Docs reconciled here (HO 478): roadmap "Also (HO 477)" (pointer → 477), backlog (this tombstone + the two edits below), oddities ×2, SKILL (`/api/health` note). Doc sweep: HO 478.

- **Amendments status/disposition model** — **CLOSED NO-GO HO 452** (probe `67ed564`). The persisted-enum model would have walked each amendment's `/actions` sub-resource to classify the silent 91% the top-level `latest_action_text` misses, at ~2× the backfill (~6,800 → ~13,600 requests). The probe (`scripts/diagnostic/amendments-actions-probe-452.ts`, stratified n=155, 0 errors) found **0% recovery**: no-top-level-action == genuinely filed-never-acted — both the general silent stratum and the decisive `119-sconres-7` vote-a-rama stratum returned 100% submit/propose-only `/actions`, empty residual (a real absence, not a classifier miss). A vote-a-rama files ~1,131 and votes on ~24; the ~24 already carry a top-level action. So the top-level scan is the disposition ceiling and the HO 448/450 display-only dot is already the complete surface — a persisted enum would persist what render computes for a doubled backfill and no coverage gain (oddities). *Orthogonal lead surfaced by the probe:* `/actions` `recordedVotes` → amendment-vote-linkage, banked separately in QUEUED (a vote feature, not disposition).

This took down BOTH the Monday `/api/cron/weekly-report` cron (#559 06-29 error 23.8s; #287 06-15 error 20.1s) and the HO 285 daily `runReportCatchup()` gen identically (throws → row stays missing → "regenerates daily, never lands"), so the catch-up net had a hole. Cold EXPLAIN + per-query cold-time from idle: all six 20s TIMEOUT → **156–1948ms**; Q6 (`json_each + GROUP BY`, the flagged re-plan fork) HELD the hint cold. On prod: weekly-report **#673 success 13.5s**; Jun 22–28 regenerated + `/reports` revalidated. **Catch-up hardening:** `/api/sync` surfaces the once-swallowed `runReportCatchup()` error two ways — `payload.reportCatchup.ok` (machine field) + `chronicErr` → `cron_runs.error_message` with a load-bearing `report-catchup-failed:` prefix — so a status-level cron-health audit catches "catch-up failing" without parsing payload JSON (the two-week silent-failure gap). Non-goals held: no schedule change, no new route/slot, no `vercel.json`/SKILL topology change, thinkingBudget + report content untouched. - ~~**Summarize cron doom loop — bounded queue read + concurrency (HO 405 probe / HO 406 fix)**~~ — SHIPPED `82fb6bc`, live on prod, drained end-to-end. **HO 405** diagnosed two co-occurring causes; **HO 406** fixed both. **Mode 1 (aborts):** HO 383 dropped the queue read's SQL LIMIT to fetch the full null set for the app-side stage-priority sort; at 1,374 nulls it became `SCAN bills USING INDEX idx_bills_update_date` over all 16,623 rows and tipped the 10s `boundedFetch` (the 07-02/06-29 55s soft-timeouts + the 06-30 20s error). Fixed with a partial index `idx_bills_summarize_queue ON bills(update_date DESC) WHERE summary IS NULL`, forced `INDEXED BY` — the statless planner mis-plans into a `MULTI-INDEX OR` on `idx_bills_is_ceremonial` unhinted (verified). Read → `SCAN … idx_bills_summarize_queue`: **5.07s cold** at full backlog, **48ms** drained (both under the 10s wall). The app-side `computeStage` priority sort is preserved — stored `stage` is stale by design (HO 383), so an SQL stage sort can't replace it; the handoff's "push priority into SQL with LIMIT" option was rejected for this reason. **Mode 3 (throughput):** serial ~1.9s/bill cleared only ~23/tick vs ~30/day inflow → a bounded **C=5** worker pool in `lib/summarize-runner.ts` (per-bill 15s AbortController unchanged, so up to C in flight at the 45s deadline still tail out ≤15s — the 45+15=60s ceiling holds), and the cron per-tick cap **50→200** so the budget is the wall. One real prod tick: **80** summarized, 0 fail/timeout, `budgetStopped`, 49.6s. **Drain:** one unbounded `npm run summarize` cleared **1,374 → 7** (the 7 were 15s per-bill timeouts, deferred 24h, retry next tick). **Two gotchas → oddities:** (1) building the partial index over 16k rows itself tipped the 10s `boundedFetch`, so `npm run migrate` aborted it — built once out-of-band with a long-timeout libsql client, `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS` no-ops thereafter. (2) the ceremonial filter added to the queue is a verified no-op (`is_ceremonial` is set only at summarize time; sync resets it to NULL), kept as harmless feed-alignment, NOT the inflow reduction the brief assumed. Non-goals held: no schedule change, no Pro upgrade, no model/prompt change. Docs reconciled here (HO 406): backlog (this tombstone + the closed OPEN LOOP, Foundation-96-no-longer-fused note) + oddities ×2. Doc/code: HO 406.

The tape froze ~1.7 days. **313** found it was **not** a data-source failure — GitHub Actions scheduled-cron *delivery* (markets-tick delivered ~20% of weekday slots, hours late, zero some days); the data layer was healthy (all 18 live symbols ticked from prod egress when the cron fired). **314** added a reliable **Vercel daily cron** `/api/cron/markets @ 30 21 * * *` (21:30 UTC, post-close, full roster) as a floor, bounding the tape to ≤~a day stale; GitHub Actions intraday crons stay as best-effort gravy on top. Manual prove-out wrote the full live roster, tape `AS OF` current, STALE cleared. Intraday-reliable upgrade / watchdog / POLY-SHUTDOWN ghost → OPEN LOOPS.

The row is well-formed and listed; no fix warranted. Not carried in any open tracker. (CLI-write revalidate gap → OPEN LOOPS, low priority.)

- ~~**Weekly-report cron reliability (HO 284 probe + 285 catch-up)**~~ — SHIPPED 2026-06-20. *(Was the HO 242 first-cron-write OPEN LOOP.)* The weekly Monday report cron chronically dropped a week on any transient hiccup — a single-shot gen with no retry. **284** corrected the premise: the "20s LLM abort" (#287) was `boundedFetch`'s 10s+10s Turso double-timeout (a DB cold-stall in `gatherReportData`), not the LLM (the report's Gemini call has no abortSignal); three independent drop modes (DB cold-stall, Gemini 503, slow-Gemini >55s). **285** added a daily catch-up in `/api/sync` (`runReportCatchup`) that regenerates the most-recent missing of the last 4 completed weeks, one per run, idempotent — "retried daily until it lands," defeating all three. Backfilled the 06-08 gap live (laws=1 intro=117 moves=60). Residual mode-specific hardening → WATCH (HO 285 residue).
- ~~**Gated-aggregate mis-plan 500s (was "cold-start 500" BLOCKER) — CLEARED via HO 277–279**~~ — DONE 2026-06-19. The `/dashboard-v2` cold-start 500 wasn't a cold-start problem at all — it was a whole CLASS of summary-gated fat-`bills` COUNT/aggregates the statless Turso planner mis-plans onto `idx_bills_is_ceremonial` (a MULTI-INDEX OR over ~every row), slow even warm + nondeterministic (112ms↔18s+). It surfaced as live 10s-abort 500s on **`/members`** (HO 277, digest 4101894172), **`/dashboard-v2`** (HO 278, the swap blocker), and **`/bills`** (HO 279). Fixed with additive covering/partial indexes + gated `INDEXED BY` hints (the planner refuses them unhinted): `idx_bills_summary_feed` (shared: getFeedStats / getCorpusStats(true) / getFeedBills COUNT), `idx_bills_summary_stage`, `idx_bills_summary_topics`, `idx_bills_sponsor_agg`. **getFeedStats was app-wide** (masthead, every HeaderBar page) — fixing it protected every inner page. Cold-verified: `/members` 0.885s, `/dashboard-v2` 5× cold 200, `/bills` 0.705s first-hit-after-idle. 9 commits (`859466a`…`b423329`). The v2→`/` swap is unblocked (→ QUEUED). Residual same-class siblings → WATCH below. Doc sweep: HO 280.

- **Homepage `/` cold-start 500** — FIXED 2026-06-14/15 (HO 241; commits `d7c2435` forced indexes + `3880c59` prewarm revert). First `/` hit after Turso idle-evicted the `bills` page cache took ~18–20s and tripped the 10s `DB_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` abort (×2 retry = 20s 500); the recompute couldn't populate the Data Cache before aborting, so it never self-healed. *Root cause:* the ~63MB `bills` table cold-scanned by ~6 homepage queries, and the **statless planner** (ANALYZE blocked on Turso, like VACUUM) grabbed `idx_bills_is_ceremonial` for the `(is_ceremonial=0 OR NULL)` OR — ~13–20s **even warm**. *Fix:* 2 covering indexes (`idx_bills_dash_stage`, `idx_bills_dash_topics`) for the aggregates + `INDEXED BY` hints on 5 feed queries forcing the existing date/news indexes. Cold `/` 500@20s → 200@~2s, **self-recovering via populate-on-read** (the cron prewarm built mid-investigation was reverted — dead problem once the queries are fast). Detour tombstoned: the prewarm + `revalidate:false` approach (it 504'd the sync route stacking cold queries behind the unbounded Gemini lead). See HO 241 ABANDONED banner + `SKILL.md` forced-index note.
